============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) MATRIX_A : 0u);\n    s.x[h] = v;\n    s.h = h1;\n    return v;\n}\n\ninline bool getBit(const Poly& p, size_t i) { return (p[i >> 6] >> (i & 63)) & 1u; }\ninline void setBit(Poly& p, size_t i) { p[i >> 6] |= uint64_t(1) << (i & 63); }\n\ninline int degree(const Poly& p) {\n    for (int w = static_cast<int>(p.size()) - 1; w >= 0; --w)\n        if (p[w]) return w * 64 + 63 - __builtin_clzll(p[w]);\n    return -1;\n}\n\n// Minimal (characteristic) polynomial of MT19937 via Berlekamp-Massey on the LSB of the raw sequence.\ninline Poly characteristicPolynomial() {\n    const int NB = 2 * DEG;\n    State s; seedState(s, 5489u);\n    // R holds the sequence reversed: bit (NB-1-n) of R = b_n\n    const int RW = NB / 64 + 2;\n    std::vector<uint64_t> R(RW, 0);\n    for (int n = 0; n < NB; ++n) {\n        uint32_t v = step(s);\n        if (v & 1u) R[(NB - 1 - n) >> 6] |= uint64_t(1) << ((NB - 1 - n) & 63);\n    }\n    auto Rword = [&](size_t bitOff) -> uint64_t {   // 64 bits of R starting at bitOff\n        size_t q = bitOff >> 6, r = bitOff & 63;\n        uint64_t lo = q < R.size() ? R[q] : 0, hi = (q + 1) < R.size() ? R[q + 1] : 0;\n        return r ? (lo >> r) | (hi << (64 - r)) : lo;\n    };\n    const int CW = NB / 64 + 2;\n    Poly C(CW, 0), B(CW, 0), T(CW, 0);\n    C[0] = 1; B[0] = 1;\n    int L = 0, m = 1;\n    for (int n = 0; n < NB; ++n) {\n        // d = sum_{i=0..L} C_i b_{n-i} = <C, R shifted by (NB-1-n)>\n        size_t off = NB - 1 - n;\n        int words = L / 64 + 1;\n        uint64_t acc = 0;\n        for (int w = 0; w < words; ++w) acc ^= C[w] & Rword(off + 64 * static_cast<size_t>(w));\n        if (!(__builtin_popcountll(acc) & 1)) { ++m; continue; }\n        // C ^= B << m\n        auto xorShift = [&](Poly& dst, const Poly& src, int sh) {\n            int wq = sh >> 6, r = sh & 63;\n            for (int w = static_cast<int>(dst.size()) - 1; w >= wq; --w) {\n                uint64_t v = src[w - wq] << r;\n                if (r && w - wq - 1 >= 0) v |= src[w - wq - 1] >> (64 - r);\n                dst[w] ^= v;\n            }\n        };\n        if (2 * L <= n) {\n            T = C;\n            xorShift(C, B, m);\n            L = n + 1 - L;\n            B = T;\n            m = 1;\n        } else {\n            xorShift(C, B, m);\n            ++m;\n        }\n    }\n    if (L != DEG) { fprintf(stderr, \"mtjump: unexpected minimal polynomial degree %d\\n\", L); std::exit(2); }\n    // phi_i = C_{L-i}\n    Poly phi(PW + 1, 0);\n    for (int i = 0; i <= L; ++i) if (getBit(C, L - i)) setBit(phi, i);\n    return phi;\n}\n\nstruct Modulus {\n    Poly phi;                                   // degree DEG\n    std::vector<uint64_t> shifted;              // 64 x TW words: phi << r\n    explicit Modulus(Poly p) : phi(std::move(p)), shifted(64 * TW, 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < 64; ++r) {\n            uint64_t* d = &shifted[r * TW];\n            for (int w = 0; w < TW; ++w) {\n                uint64_t v = 0;\n                int sw = w;  // source word\n                if (sw < static_cast<int>(phi.size())) v |= phi[sw] << r;\n                if (r && sw - 1 >= 0 && sw - 1 < static_cast<int>(phi.size())) v |= phi[sw - 1] >> (64 - r);\n                d[w] = v;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Reduce a polynomial of degree <= 2*DEG-2 (size >= 2*PW words) in place; result in low PW words\n    void reduce(Poly& p) const {\n        for (int i = static_cast<int>(p.size()) * 64 - 1; i >= DEG; --i) {\n            if (!getBit(p, static_cast<size_t>(i))) continue;\n            int sh = i - DEG, wq = sh >> 6, r = sh & 63;\n            const uint64_t* src = &shifted[r * TW];\n            int lim = std::min(TW, static_cast<int>(p.size()) - wq);\n            for (int w = 0; w < lim; ++w) p[wq + w] ^= src[w];\n        }\n        p.resize(PW);\n    }\n    Poly mul(const Poly& a, const Poly& b) const {\n        // shifted copies of a\n        std::vector<uint64_t> at(64 * TW, 0);\n        for (int r = 0; r < 64; ++r) {\n            uint64_t* d = &at[r * TW];\n            for (int w = 0; w < TW; ++w) {\n                uint64_t v = 0;\n                if (w < PW) v |= a[w] << r;\n                if (r && w - 1 >= 0 && w - 1 < PW) v |= a[w - 1] >> (64 - r);\n                d[w] = v;\n            }\n        }\n        Poly p(2 * PW + 2, 0);\n        for (int i = 0; To compute form factors in parallel while\n// reproducing exactly the same samples, every worker needs the generator state\n// at an arbitrary offset into that stream.  mt19937::discard() is O(offset),\n// which would cost as much as the actual work, so we jump ahead with the\n// standard GF(2) polynomial method: the state transition A is linear, so\n// A^v s = g(A) s with g(t) = t^v mod phi(t), where phi is the characteristic\n// polynomial of A (degree 19937, obtained via Berlekamp-Massey).  g(A) s is\n// then evaluated by Horner's rule using 19937 single steps of the recurrence.\n\nnamespace mtjump {\n\nconstexpr int MT_N = 624, MT_M = 397;\nconstexpr uint32_t MATRIX_A = 0x9908b0dfu, UPPER_MASK = 0x80000000u, LOWER_MASK = 0x7fffffffu;\nconstexpr int DEG = 19937;                 // degree of the characteristic polynomial\nconstexpr int PW = (DEG + 63) / 64;        // words for a polynomial of degree < DEG\nconstexpr int TW = PW + 2;                 // words for a shifted copy of a degree-DEG polynomial\n\nusing Poly = std::vector<uint64_t>;        // bit i = coefficient of t^i\n\n// Sliding-window state (x_n, ..., x_{n+623}): logical word k is x[(h + k) % MT_N]\nstruct State {\n    uint32_t x[MT_N];\n    int h = 0;\n};\n\n// Same initialisation as std::mt19937(seed)\ninline void seedState(State& s, uint32_t seed) {\n    s.x[0] = seed;\n    for (int i = 1; i < MT_N; ++i) {\n        uint32_t p = s.x[i - 1];\n        s.x[i] = 1812433253u * (p ^ (p >> 30)) + static_cast<uint32_t>(i);\n    }\n    s.h = 0;\n}\n\n// One recurrence step: (x_n..x_{n+623}) -> (x_{n+1}..x_{n+624}); returns x_{n+624}\ninline uint32_t step(State& s) {\n    int h = s.h;\n    int h1 = h + 1; if (h1 >= MT_N) h1 -= MT_N;\n    int hm = h + MT_M; if (hm >= MT_N) hm -= MT_N;\n    uint32_t y = (s.x[h] & UPPER_MASK) | (s.x[h1] & LOWER_MASK);\n    uint32_t v = s.x[hm] ^ (y >> 1) ^ ((y & 1u) ? MATRIX_A : 0u);\n    s.x[h] = v;\n    s.h = h1;\n    return v;\n}\n\ninline bool getBit(const Poly& p, size_t i) { return (p[i >> 6] >> (i & 63)) & 1u; }\ninline void setBit(Poly& p, size_t i) { p[i >> 6] |= uint64_t(1) << (i & 63); }\n\n// Characteristic polynomial of MT19937 via Berlekamp-Massey on the LSB of the raw sequence\ninline Poly characteristicPolynomial() {\n    const int NB = 2 * DEG;\n    State s;\n    seedState(s, 5489u);\n    // R holds the bit sequence reversed: bit (NB-1-n) of R = b_n\n    std::vector<uint64_t> R(NB / 64 + 2, 0);\n    for (int n = 0; n < NB; ++n) {\n        if (step(s) & 1u) R[(NB - 1 - n) >> 6] |= uint64_t(1) << ((NB - 1 - n) & 63);\n    }\n    auto Rword = [&](size_t bitOff) -> uint64_t {\n        size_t q = bitOff >> 6, r = bitOff & 63;\n        uint64_t lo = q < R.size() ? R[q] : 0, hi = (q + 1) < R.size() ? R[q + 1] : 0;\n        return r ? (lo >> r) | (hi << (64 - r)) : lo;\n    };\n    auto xorShift = [](Poly& dst, const Poly& src, int sh) {   // dst ^= src << sh\n        int wq = sh >> 6, r = sh & 63;\n        for (int w = static_cast<int>(dst.size()) - 1; w >= wq; --w) {\n            uint64_t v = src[w - wq] << r;\n            if (r && w - wq - 1 >= 0) v |= src[w - wq - 1] >> (64 - r);\n            dst[w] ^= v;\n        }\n    };\n    const int CW = NB / 64 + 2;\n    Poly C(CW, 0), B(CW, 0), T(CW, 0);\n    C[0] = 1; B[0] = 1;\n    int L = 0, m = 1;\n    for (int n = 0; n < NB; ++n) {\n        // discrepancy d = sum_{i=0..L} C_i b_{n-i}\n        size_t off = static_cast<size_t>(NB - 1 - n);\n        int words = L / 64 + 1;\n        uint64_t acc = 0;\n        for (int w = 0; w < words; ++w) acc ^= C[w] & Rword(off + 64 * static_cast<size_t>(w));\n        if (!(__builtin_popcountll(acc) & 1)) { ++m; continue; }\n        if (2 * L <= n) {\n            T = C;\n            xorShift(C, B, m);\n            L = n + 1 - L;\n            B = T;\n            m = 1;\n        } else {\n            xorShift(C, B, m);\n            ++m;\n        }\n    }\n    if (L != DEG) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"mtjump: unexpected minimal polynomial degree %d\\\\n\", L);\n        std::exit(2);\n    }\n    Poly phi(PW + 1, 0);   // phi_i = C_{L-i}\n    for (int i = 0; i <= L; ++i) if (getBit(C, static_cast<size_t>(L - i))) setBit(phi, static_cast<size_t>(i));\n    return phi;\n}\n\n// Arithmetic in GF(2)[t] / (phi)\nstruct Modulus {\n    Poly phi;\n    std::vector<uint64_t> shifted;   // 64 x TW words: phi << r\n\n    explicit Modulus(Poly p) : phi(std::move(p)), shifted(64 * TW, 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r < 64; ++r) {\n            uint64_t* d = &shifted[static_cast<size_t>(r) * TW];\n            for (int w = 0; w < TW; ++w) {\n                uint64_t v = 0;\n                if (w < static_cast<int>(phi.size())) v |= phi[w] << r;\n                if (r && w >= 1 && w - 1 < static_cast<int>(phi.size())) v |= phi[w - 1] >> (64 - r);\n                d[w] = v;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Reduce in place (any size); result truncated to PW words\n    void reduce(Poly& p) const {\n        for (int i = static_cast<int>(p.size()) * 64 - 1; i >= DEG; --i) {\n            if (!getBit(p, static_cast<size_t>(i))) continue;\n            int sh = i - DEG, wq = sh >> 6, r = sh & 63;\n            const uint64_t* src = &shifted[static_cast<size_t>(r) * TW];\n            int lim = std::min(TW, static_cast<int>(p.size()) - wq);\n            for (int w = 0; w < lim; ++w) p[wq + w] ^= src[w];\n        }\n        p.resize(PW);\n    }\n\n    Poly square(const Poly& a) const {\n        Poly p(2 * PW + 2, 0);\n        for (int i = 0; i < DEG; ++i)\n            if (getBit(a, static_cast<size_t>(i))) setBit(p, 2 * static_cast<size_t>(i));\n        reduce(p);\n        return p;\n    }\n\n    Poly mulT(const Poly& a) const {   // a * t mod phi\n        Poly p(PW + 1, 0);\n        for (int w = 0; w < PW; ++w) {\n            uint64_t v = a[w] << 1;\n            if (w > 0) v |= a[w - 1] >> 63;\n            p[w] = v;\n        }\n        reduce(p);\n        return p;\n    }\n\n    // t^v mod phi (left-to-right binary exponentiation: squarings and shifts only)\n    Poly powT(uint64_t v) const {\n        Poly r(PW, 0);\n        r[0] = 1;\n        if (v == 0) return r;\n        int top = 63 - __builtin_clzll(v);\n        for (int b = top; b >= 0; --b) {\n            r = square(r);\n            if ((v >> b) & 1u) r = mulT(r);\n        }\n        return r;\n    }\n};\n\n// s <- g(A) s by Horner's rule; g has degree < DEG\ninline void applyPoly(State& s, const Poly& g) {\n    // Linearise s so that logical word k is lin[k]\n    uint32_t lin[MT_N];\n    for (int k = 0; k < MT_N; ++k) {\n        int idx = s.h + k; if (idx >= MT_N) idx -= MT_N;\n        lin[k] = s.x[idx];\n    }\n    State r;\n    for (int k = 0; k < MT_N; ++k) r.x[k] = 0;\n    r.h = 0;\n    for (int i = DEG - 1; i >= 0; --i) {\n        step(r);\n        if (getBit(g, static_cast<size_t>(i))) {\n            // r ^= s, respecting r's rotation: r.x[(h+k)%N] ^= lin[k]\n            int h = r.h;\n            int first = MT_N - h;\n            for (int k = 0; k < first; ++k) r.x[h + k] ^= lin[k];\n            for (int k = first; k < MT_N; ++k) r.x[k - first] ^= lin[k];\n        }\n    }\n    s = r;\n}\n\n// std::mt19937 positioned at the given state (next output = temper(x_{n+624}))\ninline std::mt19937 toEngine(const State& s) {\n    std::stringstream ss;\n    for (int k = 0; k < MT_N; ++k) {\n        int idx = s.h + k; if (idx >= MT_N) idx -= MT_N;\n        ss << s.x[idx] << ' ';\n    }\n    ss << MT_N;\n    std::mt19937 e;\n    ss >> e;\n    return e;\n}\n\n// Engine equivalent to std::mt19937(seed) followed by discard(offset)\ninline std::mt19937 engineAt(uint32_t seed, uint64_t offset, const Modulus& mod) {\n    State s;\n    seedState(s, seed);\n    if (offset > 0) applyPoly(s, mod.powT(offset));\n    return toEngine(s);\n}\n\n} // namespace mtjump''')\n\nopen('roomsim.cpp','w').write(src)\nPYEOF\ngrep -n \"namespace mtjump\" roomsim.cpp","description":"Insert MT19937 jump-ahead code into roomsim.cpp"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5671,"cache_read_input_tokens":56070,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5671},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f28743dd-c2ea-4a84-801f-0ccca87e9433","uuid":"a2076082-ce1c-4ea3-8345-c3d539603f44","timestamp":"2026-10-30T17:12:38.127Z","request_id":"req_011CgYnyJuXjgQUegNW2FCgf"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgYpHjRnQDVAG8pEXUBiT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The roomsim benchmark is now parallelized with OpenMP in the two existing files, `roomsim/roomsim.cpp` and `roomsim/CMakeLists.txt`. Results are byte-identical to the sequential original in every configuration tested.\n\n**Performance** (2-socket EPYC 7763, 256 threads):\n\n| Config | Original | Parallel | Speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| -n 320 -t 50 | 1.47 s | 0.05 s | ~28x |\n| -n 1280 -t 50 | 26.5 s | 0.23 s | ~115x |\n| -n 5120 -t 50 | 469 s | 2.9 s | ~160x |\n\nScaling is linear at low counts (3 threads 3.0x, 7 threads 6.9x), and single-thread time equals the original. The remaining 5120 time is almost entirely the ray/octree work itself, with load imbalance and jump overhead under 2%.\n\n**What changed**\n\n- **Form factors, the dominant phase.** The original consumes one `mt19937(42)` stream sequentially across all triangle pairs. To keep bit-identical samples, each worker positions its own generator at the exact stream offset of its chunk using MT19937 jump-ahead. The characteristic polynomial is derived via Berlekamp–Massey, and t^offset mod φ is evaluated by Horner's rule. `discard()` would have cost ~2 s per thread at 5120. Draws per `rand()` call are probed at runtime rather than assumed.\n- **Load balancing.** Pairs are split into ~1000-pair chunks. Each thread owns a contiguous range, so its generator flows from chunk to chunk without jumps. Idle threads steal the back half of the largest remaining range via a single compare-and-swap, and jump only then.\n- **Octree traversal.** The tree is flattened into a contiguous node array with per-ray constants hoisted out of the box test. Same tests, same float operations, same visited set, about 7% faster.\n- **Other phases.** Time delays, wave propagation (parallel over receivers per timestep inside one region, barrier per step, unchanged summation order), cross-correlation distances, and validation counts are parallelized. Light phases scale their team size with problem size to avoid barrier overhead on small inputs.\n- **CMake** now requires OpenMP and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. No new dependencies or files, executable name unchanged.\n\n**Verified** against the original binary with `-o` output comparison and `-v` validation at 320, 320 with -t 100 -s 7 -r 0.5, 1280, and 5120, plus edge cases -n 20 and -t 0, and thread counts 1, 3, 7, 100, 128, 256. Progress lines are still printed every 100 rows, though their order may interleave slightly across threads."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":354,"cache_read_input_tokens":144298,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":354},"output_tokens":775,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f28743dd-c2ea-4a84-801f-0ccca87e9433","uuid":"32b893aa-5663-4541-850f-95ed971fb373","timestamp":"2026-10-30T17:28:40.236Z","request_id":"req_011CgYpHj5D1pq7L8YQWgAU4"}
